- **polya-heuristic-teaching**: Teach any discipline that has a "problem spine" with George Polya's heuristic method (the four steps of How to Solve It plus the classical heuristics). One small concrete problem is the entry point. The student computes small cases, is pushed to a sharp conjecture, finds the proof from structure already present in the problem, hears the moves named afterwards, and is hooked to the next rung of a 3-to-5-rung problem ladder the teacher drew before class. Solving one problem loads the field's key concepts, techniques and intuitions. Trigger when the user says "teach me X", "walk me through X", "… ([Polaris-Aeterna/skills](https://github.com/Polaris-Aeterna/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/polya-heuristic-teaching))

- **polymarket-48h-geopolitics-cluster-trader**: Trades logical inconsistencies in geopolitical event clusters on Polymarket. Geopolitical markets form clusters where probabilities must satisfy constraints — strike-count markets are cumulative (P(7) &lt;= P(6)), daily military action across regions should correlate, and prerequisite events constrain downstream markets. When these constraints are violated, the mispriced market reverts. This skill detects violations and trades the correction, sizing by conviction. ([MacD1998/SkillSecurer](https://github.com/MacD1998/SkillSecurer/tree/HEAD/dataset/SkillTrustBench/secureSkills/case_04220) · ★1 · 6 copies)
